Existing approaches typically learn a mapping from camera motion to video using additional camera modules and paired data. However, such datasets are often limited in scale, diversity, and scene dynamics, which can bias the model toward a narrow output distribution and compromise the strong prior learned by the base model. These limitations motivate a different perspective on camera control. In this paper, we show that camera control need not be modeled as an implicit mapping problem, but can instead be treated as a form of geometric guidance that induces displacements across frames. Specifically, we reformulate camera control into a set of displacement fields and apply them via differentiable resampling of latent features during denoising. Our simple approach achieves effective camera control with minimal degradation across diverse quality metrics compared to fine-tuned baselines. Since our method is applicable to most video diffusion models without training, it can also serve as a probe to study the camera control capabilities of base models. Using this probe, we identify universal biases shared by representative video models, as well as disparities in their responses to camera control. Finally, we benchmark their performance in multi-view generation, offering insights into their potential for 3D/4D tasks.

</details>


### [73] [The Velocity Deficit: Initial Energy Injection for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14819)
*Linze Li,Zong-Wei Hong,Shen Zhang,Bo Lin,Jinglun Li,Yao Tang,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现流匹配模型在高维实践中存在“速度赤字”问题：MSE目标函数系统地低估速度幅值，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形（集成滞后）。为此提出“初始能量注入”策略，包含基于训练的MAFM和无训练的SSC方法。SSC仅需一行代码即可实现零重训的性能提升：在ImageNet-1k上FID提升44.6%（13.68→7.58），实现5倍加速，50步生成器超越250步基线；在MS-COCO上FID提升~22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配理论虽保证恒定速度轨迹，但在高维实践中存在速度低估问题（速度赤字），导致生成样本无法有效到达数据流形（集成滞后现象）。现有方法忽视了这一关键不对称性：轨迹起始阶段的速度收缩会造成有害的动能停滞，而末端则是有益的去噪机制。

Method: 提出初始能量注入策略，包含两种互补方法：(1) 基于训练的幅值感知流匹配（MAFM），显式修正速度幅值估计；(2) 无训练的时间尺度调度修正器（SSC），仅需一行代码调整时间尺度调度策略，利用速度收缩在末端的有益去噪作用同时避免起始阶段的动能停滞。

Result: SSC在ImageNet-1k (256×256)上实现FID从13.68降至7.58（提升44.6%），达到5倍加速效果，使50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越250步基线（FID 8.65）。在MS-COCO文本到图像生成任务上FID提升约22%，并泛化至高分辨率生成场景。

Conclusion: 速度赤字是流匹配模型在高维实践中的关键瓶颈，初始能量注入策略通过修正时间尺度调度有效解决该问题。SSC方法以极简实现（零重训、一行代码）带来显著性能提升，为流匹配模型提供了一种高效实用的改进方案。

Abstract: While Flow Matching theoretically guarantees constant-velocity trajectories, we identify a critical breakdown in high-dimensional practice: the Velocity Deficit. We show that the MSE objective systematically underestimates velocity magnitude, causing generated samples to fail to reach the data manifold-a phenomenon we term Integration Lag. To rectify this, we propose Initial Energy Injection, instantiated via two complementary methods: the training-based Magnitude-Aware Flow Matching (MAFM) and the training-free Scale Schedule Corrector (SSC). Both are grounded in our discovery of a crucial asymmetry: velocity contraction causes harmful kinetic stagnation at the trajectory's start, yet acts as a beneficial denoising mechanism at its end. Empirically, SSC yields significant efficiency gains with zero retraining and just one line of code. On ImageNet-1k (256x256), it improves FID by 44.6% (from 13.68 to 7.58) and achieves a 5x speedup, enabling a 50-step generator (FID 7.58) to beat a 250-step baseline (FID 8.65). Furthermore, our methods generalize to Text-to-Image tasks and high-resolution generation, improving FID on MS-COCO by ~22%.

</details>


### [74] [Editor's Choice: Evaluating Abstract Intent in Image Editing through Atomic Entity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14842)
*Mor Ventura,Roy Hirsch,Yonatan Bitton,Regev Cohen,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象图像编辑的定义与分类，构建Entity-Rubrics评估框架和AbstractEdit基准数据集，系统评估11个主流模型，发现标准架构在意图保留与编辑程度间难以平衡，指出集成高级LLM文本编码器和迭代推理是改进关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准主要关注显式、字面指令，而人类自然交流中常使用抽象概念（如“情绪”），抽象指令编辑领域尚缺乏系统性研究和评估基准。

Method: 首先形式化定义抽象图像编辑及其分类体系；提出Entity-Rubrics框架，将抽象编辑分解为逐实体的细粒度评估；构建AbstractEdit基准数据集，覆盖多样化真实场景。

Result: 在AbstractEdit上评估11个主流模型，发现标准架构存在意图保持与保真度之间的失衡问题，常出现欠编辑或过编辑现象；集成高级LLM文本编码器和迭代推理能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 基于实体的评估范式可泛化为奖励模型，助力模型理解抽象交流或定位测试时批判循环中的具体失败点，为弥合机器执行与人类开放式交流之间的差距提供基础。

Abstract: Humans naturally communicate through abstract concepts like "mood". However, current image editing benchmarks focus primarily on explicit, literal commands, leaving abstract instructions largely underexplored. In this work, we first formalize the definition and taxonomy of abstract image editing. To measure instruction-following in this challenging domain, we introduce Entity-Rubrics, a framework that breaks down abstract edits into individual, entity-level assessments and achieves strong correlation with human judgment. Alongside this framework, we contribute AbstractEdit, the first benchmark dedicated to abstract image editing across diverse real-world scenes. Evaluating 11 leading models on this dataset reveals a fundamental challenge: standard architectures struggle to balance intent and preservation, commonly defaulting to under-editing or over-editing. Our analysis demonstrates that driving meaningful improvements relies heavily on integrating advanced LLM text encoders and iterative thinking. Looking forward, our entity-based paradigm can generalize beyond assessment to serve as a reward model, enable models to correctly interpret abstract communication, or highlight specific failures in test-time critique loops. Ultimately, we hope this work serves as a stepping stone toward seamless multimodal interaction, closing the gap between rigid machine execution and the natural, open-ended way humans communicate.

</details>


### [75] [MechVerse: Evaluating Physical Motion Consistency in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14843)
*Rahul Jain,Mayank Patel,Asim Unmesh,Karthik Ramani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MechVerse是一个用于评估图像到视频生成模型机械一致性的基准测试，包含21,156个合成视频片段，涵盖1,357个机械装配体，分为三个复杂度层级。实验表明当前模型在生成符合运动学约束的机械运动方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频和图生视频模型虽然能生成视觉逼真和时间连贯的视频，但往往无法生成符合运动学和几何约束的运动，特别是在机械装配体场景中，如刚性部件变形、耦合关系断裂、运动传递中断等问题普遍存在。

Method: 构建了MechVerse基准数据集，包含21,156个合成片段、1,357个机械装配体、141个类别，按运动学复杂度分为三个层级（独立关节、成对耦合、密集耦合多部件机构）。每个片段配有结构化提示，描述部件身份、静止支撑、运动组件、运动基元、方向、速度/幅度及部件间依赖关系。评估了商业、开源和微调模型，使用标准视频指标、指令跟随评分和人工判断。

Result: 当前模型能保持良好的外观和流畅性，但在生成机械可接受的运动方面表现失败，且错误率随耦合复杂度增加而上升。

Conclusion: MechVerse为衡量和改进从图像和语言输入生成机械感知视频的能力提供了标准基准。

Abstract: Text- and image-conditioned video generation models have achieved strong visual fidelity and temporal coherence, but they often fail to generate motion governed by kinematic and geometric constraints. In these settings, object parts must remain rigid, maintain contact or coupling with neighboring components, and transfer motion consistently across connected parts. These requirements are especially explicit in articulated mechanical assemblies, where motion is constrained by rigid-link geometry, contact/coupling relations, and transmission through kinematic chains. A generated video may therefore appear plausible while violating the intended mechanism, such as rotating a part that should translate, deforming a rigid component, breaking coupling between parts, or failing to move downstream components. To evaluate this gap, We introduce MechVerse, a benchmark for mechanically consistent image-to-video generation. MechVerse contains 21,156 synthetic clips from 1,357 mechanical assemblies across 141 categories, organized into three tiers of increasing kinematic complexity: independent articulation, pairwise coupling, and densely coupled multi-part mechanisms. Each clip is paired with a structured prompt describing part identities, stationary supports, moving components, motion primitives, direction, speed/extent, and inter-part dependencies. We evaluate proprietary, open-source, and fine-tuned image-to-video models using standard video metrics, instruction-following scores, and human judgments of motion correctness and kinematic coupling. Results show that current models can preserve appearance and smoothness while failing to generate mechanically admissible motion, with errors increasing as coupling complexity grows. MechVerse provides a benchmark for measuring and improving mechanism-aware video generation from image and language inputs.

</details>


### [76] [Exploring Vision-Language Models for Online Signature Verification: A Zero-Shot Capability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14845)
*Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana,Javier Ortega-Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro等先进视觉-语言模型在签名验证任务上的零样本表现，通过将时序运动数据转换为静态图像，并引入基于令牌概率的打分协议。实验表明，模型在随机伪造场景下表现卓越（EER低至0.32%），但在熟练伪造场景中表现显著下降，且链式思维推理会引发"合理化陷阱"——模型产生运动学幻觉，将伪造痕迹解释为自然变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在通用视觉推理中表现强劲，但其在严格生物特征任务（如签名验证）中的适用性尚未被探索。作者希望填补这一空白，探究VLM能否在零样本设置下胜任专业的签名真伪鉴别任务。

Method: 1) 将原始运动时序数据转换为静态图像，在可用时利用笔画透明度编码压力信息；2) 提出新的评分协议，提取潜在令牌概率以计算稳健的生物特征分数；3) 在SVC基准上评估GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro的零样本性能；4) 分析不同伪造类型（随机伪造vs熟练伪造）下的性能差异，并研究链式思维推理的影响。

Result: 随机伪造场景下性能卓越，GPT-5.2在移动任务上EER达0.32%，超越有监督SOTA系统；熟练伪造场景下性能显著下降，且CoT推理会导致性能退化，模型产生运动学幻觉来自圆其说（"Rationalization Trap"）。

Conclusion: VLM在随机伪造签名验证中具备零样本超SOTA潜力，但在熟练伪造场景中面临根本性限制；CoT推理在生物特征任务中可能适得其反，提出了"合理化陷阱"这一关键发现，为VLM在严格安全场景中的部署提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general visual reasoning, yet their applicability to rigorous biometric tasks remains unexplored. This work presents an exploratory study evaluating the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art VLMs (GPT-5.2 and Gemini 2.5 Pro) on the Signature Verification Challenge (SVC) benchmark. To enable visual processing, raw kinematic time-series are converted into static images, encoding pressure information into stroke opacity whenever available in the source data. Furthermore, we introduce a scoring protocol that extracts latent token probabilities to compute robust biometric scores. Experimental results reveal a significant performance dichotomy dependent on signal quality and forgery type. In random forgery scenarios, the zero-shot VLM achieves exceptional discrimination, with GPT-5.2 reaching an Equal Error Rate of 0.32% in mobile tasks, outperforming supervised state-of-the-art systems. Conversely, in skilled forgery scenarios, where the task is more challenging because both signatures are almost identical, the results are significantly worse, and a critical "Rationalization Trap" emerges: chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning degrades performance as the model produces kinematic hallucinations to justify forgery artifacts as natural variability.

</details>


### [77] [SR-Prominence: A Crowdsourced Protocol and Dataset Suite for Perceptually-Weighted Super-Resolution Artifact Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14847)
*Ivan Molodetskikh,Kirill Malyshev,Mark Mirgaleev,Nikita Zagainov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"伪影显著性"作为图像超分辨率中视觉伪影的评估目标，构建了包含3,935个伪影掩码的SR-Prominence数据集，通过众包标注衡量伪影被观众注意到的比例。研究发现传统全参考指标（如SSIM、DISTS）在局部显著性信号上表现优异，而无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器泛化能力不足。48.2%的实验室标注伪影实际上不被多数观众察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法生成的图像虽细节丰富但常引入视觉伪影，而现有检测方法对所有伪影一视同仁，忽视了它们在感知影响上的巨大差异——有的几乎不可察觉，有的则非常令人不适。因此需要从二元缺陷检测转向感知影响的量化评估。

Method: 1) 提出"伪影显著性"定义：认为某区域包含明显伪影的观众比例；2) 设计众包标注协议，构建SR-Prominence数据集（含DeSRA、Open Images、Urban100及无真实参考的Urban100-HR共3,935个伪影掩码）；3) 重新标注DeSRA发现48.2%的二元伪影不被多数观众察觉；4) 全面审计现有伪影检测器、图像质量指标和SR方法；5) 发布客观评分协议，允许新指标无需进一步众包即可基准测试。

Result: 经典全参考指标（尤其是SSIM和DISTS）在局部伪影显著性信号上表现惊人地好；无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器跨数据集和参考设置泛化能力差；48.2%的DeSRA二元伪影不被多数观众注意。

Conclusion: SR伪影评估应从二元缺陷检测转向感知影响评估，SR-Prominence数据集和协议为这一转变提供了基础。全参考指标在评估伪影显著性方面比预期更有效，而无参考方法仍需改进。

Abstract: Modern image super-resolution methods generate detailed, visually appealing results, but they often introduce visual artifacts: unnatural patterns and texture distortions that degrade perceived quality. These defects vary widely in perceptual impact--some are barely noticeable, while others are highly disturbing--yet existing detection methods treat them equally. We propose artifact prominence as an evaluative target, defined as the fraction of viewers who judge a highlighted region to contain a noticeable artifact. We design a crowdsourced annotation protocol and construct SR-Prominence, a dataset suite containing 3,935 artifact masks from DeSRA, Open Images, Urban100, and a realistic no-ground-truth Urban100-HR setting, annotated with prominence. Re-annotating DeSRA reveals that 48.2% of its in-lab binary artifacts are not noticed by a majority of viewers. Across the suite, we audit SR artifact detectors, image-quality metrics, and SR methods. We find that classical full-reference metrics, especially SSIM and DISTS, provide surprisingly strong localized prominence signals, whereas no-reference IQA methods and specialized artifact detectors often fail to generalize across datasets and reference settings. SR-Prominence is released with an objective scoring protocol that allows new metrics to be benchmarked on our suite without further crowdsourcing. Together, the data and protocols enable SR artifact evaluation to move from binary defect presence toward perceptual impact. SR-Prominence is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/imolodetskikh/sr-artifact-prominence.

</details>


### [78] [FactorizedHMR: A Hybrid Framework for Video Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854)
*Patrick Kwon,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FactorizedHMR将人体网格恢复分解为确定性躯干-根节点回归和概率性流匹配肢体补全两个阶段，结合几何感知监督和特征感知无分类器引导，在遮挡和弱深度线索下显著提升模糊关节恢复的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体网格恢复存在固有歧义性：在遮挡或弱深度线索下，多个3D人体可以解释同一图像证据。且这种歧义在身体各部位分布不均——躯干姿态和根节点结构相对确定，而四肢等远端关节更不确定。现有方法未针对这一非均匀歧义特性进行差异化处理。

Method: 提出两阶段框架FactorizedHMR：1) 确定性回归模块恢复稳定的躯干-根节点锚点；2) 概率性流匹配模块补全剩余非躯干关节。结合复合目标表征、几何感知监督和特征感知无分类器引导，保持躯干-根节点锚点的同时改善模糊关节的单参考恢复。同时引入合成数据管线提供多视角下的成对图像-相机-运动监督。

Result: 在相机空间和世界空间基准上，FactorizedHMR与强基线方法竞争力相当，在重度遮挡恢复和漂移敏感的世界空间指标上优势最明显。

Conclusion: 基于身体部位歧义非均匀分布特性，将确定性与概率性建模相结合的分治策略能有效提升人体网格恢复在复杂场景下的鲁棒性，尤其在遮挡和时序漂移场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Human Mesh Recovery (HMR) is fundamentally ambiguous: under occlusion or weak depth cues, multiple 3D bodies can explain the same image evidence. This ambiguity is not uniform across the body, as torso pose and root structure are often relatively well constrained, whereas distal articulations such as the arms and legs are more uncertain. Building on this observation, we propose FactorizedHMR, a two-stage framework that treats these two regimes differently. A deterministic regression module first recovers a stable torso-root anchor, and a probabilistic flow-matching module then completes the remaining non-torso articulation. To make this completion reliable, we combine a composite target representation with geometry-aware supervision and feature-aware classifier-free guidance, preserving the torso-root anchor while improving single-reference recovery of ambiguity-prone articulation. We also introduce a synthetic data pipeline that provides the paired image-camera-motion supervision under diverse viewpoints. Across camera-space and world-space benchmarks, FactorizedHMR remains competitive with strong baselines, with the clearest gains in occlusion-heavy recovery and drift-sensitive world-space metrics.

</details>


### [79] [LPH-VTON: Resolving the Structure-Texture Dilemma of Virtual Try-On via Latent Process Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14874)
*Yixin Liu,Baihong Qian,Jinglin Jiang,Jeffery Wu,Yan Chen,Wei Wang,Yida Wang,Lanqing Yang,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LPH-VTON通过时间维度的架构解耦，在单一连续去噪过程中先后利用结构偏好模型和纹理偏好模型，解决了虚拟试穿中结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的根本性权衡，在VITON-HD数据集上实现了帕累托最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的虚拟试穿方法面临结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的根本性权衡：依赖空间约束的模型擅长几何对齐但抑制纹理，而依赖无约束生成先验的模型擅长细节渲染但易产生结构漂移。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出LPH-VTON协同框架，在单一连续去噪过程中进行策略性分解：早期阶段使用结构偏好模型建立几何一致的潜在支架，随后切换至纹理偏好模型进行高保真细节渲染，实现时间维度的架构解耦。

Result: 在VITON-HD标准数据集上，模型实现了优越的帕累托最优平衡，在感知保真度方面建立了新基准，同时保持了极具竞争力的结构对齐性能。

Conclusion: 时间维度的架构解耦有效解决了虚拟试穿中的结构-纹理权衡问题，证明了该策略的有效性。

Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to synthesize photorealistic images of garments precisely aligned with a person's body and pose. Current diffusion-based methods, however, face a fundamental trade-off between structural integrity and textural fidelity. In this paper, we formalize this challenge as a consequence of complementary inductive biases inherent in prevailing architectures: models heavily reliant on spatial constraints naturally favor geometric alignment but often suppress textures, whereas models dominated by unconstrained generative priors excel at vibrant detail rendering but are prone to structural drift. Based on this diagnosis, we propose LPH-VTON, a new synergistic framework that resolves this tension within a single, continuous denoising process. LPH-VTON strategically decomposes the generation, leveraging a structure-biased model to establish a geometrically consistent latent scaffold in the early stages, before handing over control to a texture-biased model for high-fidelity detail rendering. Extensive experiments validate our approach. Our model achieves a superior Pareto-optimal balance, establishing new benchmarks in perceptual faithfulness while maintaining highly competitive structural alignment across the standard dataset VITON-HD, proving the efficacy of temporal architectural decoupling.

</details>


### [80] [HeatKV: Head-tuned KV-cache Compression for Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14877)
*Jonathan Cederlund,Axel Berg,Durmus Alp Emre Acar,Chuteng Zhou,Pontus Giselsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatKV提出了一种针对视觉自回归（VAR）模型的新型KV缓存压缩方法，通过基于注意力得分的头级别缓存分配策略，在Infinity-2B模型上实现了2倍于现有方法的压缩比，同时保持或提升了图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR模型在图像生成中表现出色，但存在严重的KV缓存内存瓶颈，每张生成图像需要数GB内存，限制了其实际部署效率。

Method: 利用小型离线校准集计算每个注意力头对先前生成尺度的注意力得分，据此对注意力头进行排序，然后基于排序结果和给定内存预算构建静态剪枝调度策略，实现头级别（head-specific）的差异化缓存分配。

Result: 在Infinity-2B模型上，HeatKV相比现有方法实现了2倍更高的KV缓存压缩比，同时在图像保真度、提示对齐和人类感知评分上保持或更优。

Conclusion: HeatKV在VAR模型KV缓存压缩任务上达到了新的SOTA，证明了细粒度、逐头定制的缓存分配策略的有效性。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models have recently demonstrated impressive image generation quality while maintaining low latency. However, they suffer from severe KV-cache memory constraints, often requiring gigabytes of memory per generated image. We introduce HeatKV, a novel compression method that adapts cache allocation in each head based on its attention to previously generated scales. Using a small offline calibration set, the attention heads are ranked according to their attention scores over prior scales. Based on this ranking, we construct a static pruning schedule tailored to a given memory budget. Applied to the Infinity-2B model, HeatKV achieves $2 \times$ higher compression ratio in memory allocation for KV cache compared to existing methods, while maintaining similar or better image fidelity, prompt alignment and human perception score. Our method achieves a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) for VAR model KV-cache compression, showcasing the effectiveness of fine-grained, head-specific cache allocation.

</details>


### [81] [Denoising-GS: Gaussian Splatting with Spatial-aware Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14880)
*Qingyuan Zhou,Xinyi Liu,Weidong Yang,Ning Wang,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei,Ying He,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Denoising-GS，将3D高斯泼溅优化视为基元去噪过程，通过空间感知的去噪框架同时考虑位置和空间结构，在三个基准数据集上取得了最先进的新视角合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在优化过程中由于SfM点云的稀疏和不完整初始化，不可避免地引入噪声高斯基元；且大多数方法仅关注调整基元位置，忽略了潜在的空间结构。

Method: 1) 设计保留基元空间优化流的优化器；2) 空间梯度去噪策略联合考虑基元空间支持以保持梯度一致性更新；3) 不确定性去噪模块估计基元级不确定性以剪枝冗余/噪声基元；4) 空间一致性细化策略在稀疏区域选择性分裂基元以维持结构完整性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Denoising-GS一致提升了NVS保真度并保持了表示的紧凑性，达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 将3DGS优化重新定义为基元去噪过程，并引入空间感知去噪框架，有效解决了噪声基元问题，同时提升了渲染质量和模型紧凑性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable success in high-fidelity Novel View Synthesis (NVS), yet the optimization process inevitably introduces noisy Gaussian primitives due to the sparse and incomplete initialization from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) point clouds. Most existing methods focus solely on adjusting the positions of primitives during optimization, while neglecting the underlying spatial structure. To this end, we introduce a new perspective by formulating the optimization of 3DGS as a primitive denoising process and propose Denoising-GS, a spatial-aware denoising framework for Gaussian primitives by taking both the positions and spatial structure into consideration. Specifically, we design an optimizer that preserves the spatial optimization flow of primitives, facilitating coherent and directed denoising rather than random perturbations. Building upon this, the Spatial Gradient-based Denoising strategy jointly considers the spatial supports of primitives to ensure gradient-consistent updates. Furthermore, the Uncertainty-based Denoising module estimates primitive-wise uncertainty to prune redundant or noisy primitives, while the Spatial Coherence Refinement strategy selectively splits primitives in sparse regions to maintain structural completeness. Experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that Denoising-GS consistently enhances NVS fidelity while maintaining representation compactness, achieving state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [82] [Masked Next-Scale Prediction for Self-supervised Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14885)
*Zhuohao Chen,Zeng Li,Yifei Zhang,Chang Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MNSP是一种统一的自监督框架，通过掩码下一尺度预测（Masked Next-Scale Prediction）显式建模场景文本的跨尺度结构演化，在Union14M基准上达到86.2%平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MIM）仅在单一空间尺度上操作，无法捕捉场景文本从粗粒度布局到细粒度笔画的分层结构特性，且朴素尺度预测会导致注意力扩散到背景区域而非文本结构。

Method: 提出MNSP框架，包含三个核心组件：1）Next-Scale Prediction (NSP) 从低分辨率上下文预测高分辨率特征以学习分层表示；2）联合学习跨尺度预测与掩码图像重建，NSP捕获全局布局先验，掩码重建施加局部约束引导注意力聚焦文本区域；3）多尺度语言对齐模块维护不同分辨率间的语义一致性。

Result: 在Union14M基准上达到86.2%平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%，在极端尺度和布局变化下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MNSP通过显式建模跨尺度结构演化，有效解决了场景文本识别中的层次结构学习问题，达到了当前最优性能。

Abstract: Scene Text Recognition requires modeling visual structures that evolve from coarse layouts to fine-grained character strokes. Training such models relies on large amounts of annotated data. Recent self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Image Modeling (MIM), alleviate this dependency by leveraging large-scale unlabeled data. Yet most existing MIM methods operate at a single spatial scale and fail to capture the hierarchical nature of scene text. In this work, we introduce Masked Next-Scale Prediction (MNSP), a unified self-supervised framework designed to explicitly model cross-scale structural evolution. The framework incorporates Next-Scale Prediction (NSP), which learns hierarchical representations by predicting higher-resolution features from lower-resolution contexts. Naive scale prediction, however, tends to produce spatially diffuse attention, directing the model toward background regions rather than textual structures. MNSP resolves this limitation by jointly learning cross-scale prediction and masked image reconstruction. NSP captures global layout priors across resolutions, while masked reconstruction imposes strong local constraints that guide attention toward informative text regions. A Multi-scale Linguistic Alignment module further maintains semantic consistency across different resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MNSP achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 86.2\% average accuracy on the challenging Union14M benchmark and 96.7\% across six standard datasets. Additional analyses show that our method improves robustness under extreme scale and layout variations. Code is available at https://github.com/CzhczhcHczh/MNSP

</details>


### [83] [Your CLIP has 164 dimensions of noise: Exploring the embeddings covariance eigenspectrum of contrastively pretrained vision-language transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14893)
*Jakub Grzywaczewski,Dawid Płudowski,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过协方差矩阵的谱分解，将对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号成分和共享噪声子空间，发现噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间呈现强子群不变性，且剪除这些共享噪声维度对下游任务性能无害甚至有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比预训练的VLM虽然作为强大的特征提取器，但其共享潜在空间存在结构异常，并积累了非语义的多模态噪声，阻碍了对真实语义表征的理解。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵的谱分解方法，将VLM潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号成分和共享噪声子空间，并分析噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间的子群不变性。

Result: 噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间表现出强子群不变性；剪除这些共享噪声维度对下游任务性能无害甚至有益。

Conclusion: VLM潜在几何结构中有相当一部分由共享的架构级噪声主导，而非仅由任务相关的语义决定；通过隔离真实语义信号与伪影噪声，为理解现代VLM的表征结构提供了新的机制性见解。

Abstract: Contrastively pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) serve as powerful feature extractors. Yet, their shared latent spaces are prone to structural anomalies and act as repositories for non-semantic, multi-modal noise. To address this phenomenon, we employ spectral decomposition of covariance matrices to decompose the VLM latent space into a multi-modal semantic signal component and a shared noise subspace. We observe that this noise geometry exhibits strong subgroup invariance across distinct data subsets. Crucially, pruning these shared noise dimensions is mainly harmless, preserving or actively improving downstream task performance. By isolating true semantic signals from artifactual noise, this work provides new mechanistic insights into the representational structure of modern VLMs, suggesting that a substantial fraction of their latent geometry is governed by shared, architecture-level noise rather than task-relevant semantics alone.

</details>


### [84] [SEDiT: Mask-Free Video Subtitle Erasure via One-step Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14894)
*Zheng Hui,Yunlong Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEDiT提出了一种基于一步扩散Transformer的无掩码视频字幕擦除方法，通过理论证明一步采样的可行性，并采用混合训练策略和分块流式推理实现高分辨率长视频的高效处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法依赖基于掩码的修复，需要预先提取目标视频掩码，分割精度直接影响完成质量，且两阶段处理存在次优性。

Method: 提出SEDiT框架：(1) 无掩码推理，直接擦除目标字幕；(2) 一步扩散Transformer，利用字幕去除的局部编辑特性，在整流流下实现一步生成；(3) 理论证明条件最优传输映射和整流流速度场关于潜变量的Lipschitz连续性；(4) 混合训练策略，偶尔以干净首帧潜变量为条件，促进时序连续性；(5) 分块流式推理，直接处理原始视频避免拼接伪影。

Result: 方法可高效处理原生1440p视频及无限长度视频，实现高质量的字幕擦除。

Conclusion: SEDiT通过一步扩散Transformer和无掩码推理，克服了传统两阶段方法的局限，在保持高质量的同时显著提升了视频字幕擦除的效率，尤其适用于高分辨率和长视频场景。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in video diffusion models have significantly accelerated the development of video editing techniques. However, existing methods often rely on inpainting video frames based on masked input, which requires extracting the target video mask in advance, and the precision of the segmentation directly affects the quality of the completion. In this paper, we present SEDiT, a novel one-stage video Subtitle Erasure approach via One-step Diffusion Transformer. We introduce a mask-free inference approach that enables direct erasure of the targeted subtitle. The proposed one-stage framework mitigates the sub-optimality inherent in the two-stage processing of prior models. Since subtitle removal is a localized editing task in which most pixels remain unchanged, the underlying distribution shift is minimal, making it well-suited to one-step generation under rectified flow. We empirically validate the reliability of one-step denoising and further provide a formal theoretical justification. Under the localized-editing structure of subtitle removal, the conditional optimal transport (OT) map and its induced rectified flow velocity field are Lipschitz continuous with respect to the latent variable, which underpins the theoretical feasibility of one-step sampling. To address the challenge of long-term temporal consistency, we adopt a hybrid training strategy by occasionally conditioning the model with a clean first-frame latent. This facilitates temporal continuity, allowing each segment during inference to leverage the output of its predecessor. To avoid visible seams caused by cropping and reinserting processed targets, particularly in scenarios involving substantial motion, we feed the original video directly into SEDiT. Thanks to one-step and chunk-wise streaming inference, our method can efficiently handle native 1440p video with infinite length.

</details>


### [85] [MemLens: Benchmarking Multimodal Long-Term Memory in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14906)
*Xiyu Ren,Zhaowei Wang,Yiming Du,Zhongwei Xie,Chi Liu,Xinlin Yang,Haoyue Feng,Wenjun Pan,Tianshi Zheng,Baixuan Xu,Zhengnan Li,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEMLENS是一个多模态多会话对话记忆基准测试，包含789个问题、5种记忆能力、4种上下文长度。评估27个LVLM和7个记忆增强智能体后发现：长上下文LVLM在短上下文中表现好但随长度增长下降，记忆智能体长度稳定但视觉保真度差，多会话推理准确率普遍低于30%，两种方法均无法独立解决任务，需要混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对长上下文LVLM和记忆增强智能体在多模态证据需求场景下的系统比较，无法评估两者在多模态多会话对话中的记忆能力。

Method: 构建MEMLENS基准，包含789个问题、5种记忆能力（信息提取、多会话推理、时间推理、知识更新、拒绝回答）、4种上下文长度（32K-256K tokens），采用跨模态token计数方案，并设计图像消融实验验证视觉证据必要性。

Result: 图像消融实验：移除证据图像后，前沿LVLM准确率降至2%以下。长上下文LVLM短上下文准确率高但随对话增长下降；记忆智能体长度稳定但存储压缩导致视觉保真度损失；多会话推理多数系统低于30%。

Conclusion: 两种方法均无法独立解决多模态记忆任务，需要结合长上下文注意力与结构化多模态检索的混合架构。

Abstract: Memory is essential for large vision-language models (LVLMs) to handle long, multimodal interactions, with two method directions providing this capability: long-context LVLMs and memory-augmented agents. However, no existing benchmark conducts a systematic comparison of the two on questions that genuinely require multimodal evidence. To close this gap, we introduce MEMLENS, a comprehensive benchmark for memory in multimodal multi-session conversations, comprising 789 questions across five memory abilities (information extraction, multi-session reasoning, temporal reasoning, knowledge update, and answer refusal) at four standard context lengths (32K-256K tokens) under a cross-modal token-counting scheme. An image-ablation study confirms that solving MEMLENS requires visual evidence: removing evidence images drops two frontier LVLMs below 2% accuracy on the 80.4% of questions whose evidence includes images. Evaluating 27 LVLMs and 7 memory-augmented agents, we find that long-context LVLMs achieve high short-context accuracy through direct visual grounding but degrade as conversations grow, whereas memory agents are length-stable but lose visual fidelity under storage-time compression. Multi-session reasoning caps most systems below 30%, and neither approach alone solves the task. These results motivate hybrid architectures that combine long-context attention with structured multimodal retrieval. Our code is available at https://github.com/xrenaf/MEMLENS.

</details>


### [86] [SteerSeg: Attention Steering for Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14908)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Abdelwahed Khamis,Morteza Saberi,Aijun An,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteerSeg 是一个轻量级框架，通过可学习的软提示和Chain-of-Thought提示来引导大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的注意力分布，从而改善视频推理分割中的空间定位能力。该方法冻结LVLM和分割模型参数，仅学习少量软提示，在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练后能泛化到多种基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法利用冻结的LVLM提取注意力图作为空间先验进行分割，但注意力图是为文本生成而非空间定位优化的，导致定位信号模糊、不准确。论文识别出注意力错位（attention misalignment）是这一范式的关键瓶颈。

Method: SteerSeg 结合可学习的软提示（soft prompts）和推理引导的Chain-of-Thought提示。软提示重塑注意力分布以产生更集中的注意力图；CoT属性通过引导注意力指向正确实例来消除相似对象间的歧义。将处理后的注意力图转换为关键帧上的点提示以引导分割模型，并通过相关性评分排序和选择候选跟踪片段。

Result: 在仅使用Ref-YouTube-VOS训练的情况下，SteerSeg在多个视频推理分割基准上表现优异，显著提升了LVLM的空间定位能力，并展现出良好的跨数据集泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过输入级条件控制从源头引导注意力，是一种高效且可泛化的改善LVLM空间定位能力的方法，无需修改模型参数或增加大量计算开销。

Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation requires localizing objects across video frames from natural language expressions, often involving spatial reasoning and implicit references. Recent approaches leverage frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) by extracting attention maps and using them as spatial priors for segmentation, enabling training-free grounding. However, these attention maps are optimized for text generation rather than spatial localization, often resulting in diffuse and ambiguous grounding signals. In this work, we introduce SteerSeg, a lightweight framework that identifies attention misalignment as the key bottleneck in attention-based grounding and proposes to steer attention at its source through input-level conditioning. SteerSeg combines learnable soft prompts with reasoning-guided Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting. The soft prompts reshape the attention distribution to produce more spatially concentrated maps, while CoT-derived attributes resolve ambiguity among similar objects by guiding attention toward the correct instance. The resulting attention maps are converted into point prompts across keyframes to guide a segmentation model, while candidate tracklets are ranked and selected using correlation-based scoring. Our approach freezes the LVLM and segmentation model parameters and learns only a small set of soft prompts, preserving the model's pretrained reasoning capabilities while significantly improving grounding. Despite being trained only on Ref-YouTube-VOS, SteerSeg generalizes well across diverse benchmarks, significantly improving the spatial grounding capability of LVLMs. Project page: https://steerseg.github.io

</details>


### [87] [SceneParser: Hierarchical Scene Parsing for Visual Semantics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14923)
*Pengxin Xu,Xincheng Lin,Luping Xiao,Qing Jiang,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Shanghang Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化场景解析（Hierarchical Scene Parsing）任务及其对应的SceneParser模型和SceneParser-Bench基准，旨在将物理场景表示为场景→物体→部件→功能（affordance）的清晰层次结构，并实现跨层级绑定。实验表明现有模型在该任务上表现不佳，而SceneParser在结构感知性能上更优，且兼容传统任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景感知工作（如开放词汇定位、部件定位、功能预测）多为孤立的预测任务，缺乏捕捉交互导向场景理解所必需的层次化结构化依赖关系。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出以交互为导向的层次化场景解析任务。

Method: 提出SceneParser，一种基于VLM的解析器，通过结构补全伪标签和课程学习进行统一的层次化生成训练。构建SceneParser-Bench，包含11万训练图像、5000验证图像、77.7万物体、114万部件、174万功能标注及174万有效物体-部件-功能链实例。引入Level-1到Level-3条件指标和ParseRate来评估定位、跨层级绑定和层次完整性。

Result: 现有MLLMs和感知拼接流水线在SceneParser-Bench上难以完成层次化解析，而SceneParser取得了更强的结构感知性能。此外，在COCO和AGD20K上的评估及下游规划探针实验表明SceneParser兼容传统任务并提供可操作的表征。

Conclusion: 层次化场景解析是一种有效的交互导向场景理解范式，SceneParser方法在结构感知性能上优于现有方案，且能够兼容传统视觉任务，为视觉理解提供可操作的表征。

Abstract: General scene perception has progressed from object recognition toward open-vocabulary grounding, part localization, and affordance prediction. Yet these capabilities are often realized as isolated predictions that localize objects, parts, or interaction points without capturing the structured dependencies needed for interaction-oriented scene understanding. To address this gap, we introduce Hierarchical Scene Parsing, an interaction-oriented parsing task that represents physical scenes as explicit scene -> object -> part -> affordance hierarchies with cross-level bindings. We instantiate this task with SceneParser, a VLM-based parser trained for unified hierarchical generation with structural-completion pseudo labels and curriculum learning. To support training and evaluation, we construct SceneParser-Bench, a large-scale benchmark built with a scalable hierarchical data engine, containing 110K training images, a 5K validation split, 777K objects, 1.14M parts, 1.74M affordance annotations, and 1.74M valid object-part-affordance chain instances. We further introduce Level-1 to Level-3 conditional metrics and ParseRate to evaluate localization, cross-level binding, and hierarchical completeness. Experiments show that existing MLLMs and perception-stitching pipelines struggle with hierarchical parsing on our SceneParser-Bench, while SceneParser achieves stronger structure-aware performance. Besides, ablations, evaluations on COCO and AGD20K, and a downstream planning probe demonstrate that our SceneParser is compatible with conventional tasks and provides an actionable representation for visual understanding.

</details>


### [88] [Multi-scale Coarse-to-fine Modeling for Test-time Human Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14935)
*Nhat Le,Daochang Liu,Anh Nguyen,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSCoT，一种多尺度粗到细框架，用于测试时人体运动合成与控制。通过离散化运动为多尺度层次表示、粗到细的token序列预测、多尺度token引导策略以及轻量级token精炼器，实现高质量、高控制精度且速度比扩散方法快10倍的运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖多步迭代去噪/令牌预测或针对特定控制信号的定制模块，导致推理速度慢且灵活性不足。

Method: 1) 将运动离散化为多尺度层次表示，按粗到细方式预测各时间尺度的完整token序列；2) 提出多尺度token引导策略，通过引导token分布朝向控制目标实现灵活控制；3) 引入轻量级token精炼器，对离散token嵌入添加连续残差，支持可微的测试时精炼优化以确保精准对齐控制目标。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，FID提升48%，平均控制误差降低61%，推理速度比扩散方法快10倍。

Conclusion: MSCoT在可控文本到运动生成任务上达到最优性能，兼顾运动质量、控制精度与推理效率。

Abstract: We present MSCoT, a multi-scale, coarse-to-fine model for test-time human motion synthesis and control. Unlike recent approaches that rely on multiple iterative denoising/token-prediction steps, or modules tailored for specific control signals, MSCoT discretizes motion into a multi-scale hierarchical representation and predicts the entire token sequence at each temporal scale in a coarse-to-fine fashion. Building on this coarse-to-fine paradigm, we propose an efficient multi-scale token guidance strategy that overcomes the challenge of discrete sampling and steers the token distribution towards the control goals, allowing for fast and flexible control. To address the limitations of a discrete codebook, a lightweight token refiner further adds continuous residuals to the discrete token embeddings and allows differentiable test-time refinement optimization to ensure precise alignment with the control objectives. MSCoT is able to produce quality motions, consistent with the control constraints, while offering substantially faster sampling than diffusion-based approaches. Experiments on popular benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art controllable text-to-motion generation performance of MSCoT over existing baselines, with better motion quality (48% FID improvement), higher control accuracy (-61% avg error), and $10 \times$ faster inference speed on HumanML3D.

</details>


### [89] [ACE-LoRA: Adaptive Orthogonal Decoupling for Continual Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14948)
*Yuehao Liu,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Zhizhou Chen,Yanhao Ge,Shanyan Guan,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE-LoRA框架，通过自适应正交解耦和秩不变历史信息压缩策略解决持续图像编辑中的灾难性遗忘问题，并构建了首个标准化基准数据集CIE-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中的图像编辑需要持续适应新任务同时保持已学知识，但持续学习在图像编辑领域尚未被充分探索，且缺乏标准化评估协议。

Method: 提出ACE-LoRA动态正则化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自适应正交解耦（Adaptive Orthogonal Decoupling）用于识别和正交化任务间干扰；(2) 秩不变历史信息压缩策略（Rank-Invariant Historical Information Compression）解决持续更新中的可扩展性问题。同时构建了首个综合基准数据集CIE-Bench。

Result: 实验表明ACE-LoRA在指令保真度、视觉真实性和抗遗忘鲁棒性方面持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ACE-LoRA为持续图像编辑建立了坚实基础，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，为该领域提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: State-of-the-art diffusion models often rely on parameter-efficient fine-tuning to perform specialized image editing tasks. However, real-world applications require continual adaptation to new tasks while preserving previously learned knowledge. Despite the practical necessity, continual learning for image editing remains largely underexplored. We propose ACE-LoRA, a dynamic regularization framework for continual image editing that effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting. ACE-LoRA leverages Adaptive Orthogonal Decoupling to identify and orthogonalize task interference, and introduces a Rank-Invariant Historical Information Compression strategy to address scalability issues in continual updates. To facilitate continual learning in image editing and provide a standardized evaluation protocol, we introduce CIE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark in this domain. CIE-Bench encompasses diverse and practically relevant image editing scenarios with a balanced level of difficulty to effectively expose limitations of existing models while remaining compatible with parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of instruction fidelity, visual realism, and robustness to forgetting, establishing a strong foundation for continual learning in image editing.

</details>


### [90] [A CUBS-Compatible Ultrasound Morphology and Uncertainty-Aware Baseline for Carotid Intima-Media Segmentation and Preliminary Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14949)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AtheroFlow-XNet，一种基于超声形态学与不确定性感知学习的颈动脉内中膜分割与风险预测基线模型，在CUBS数据集上实现了0.7930的Dice系数和0.6910的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统颈动脉超声评估依赖形态学与流速指标，无法全面捕捉患者特异性血管风险，亟需一种自动化、可解释且具备不确定性感知的分析方法。

Method: 基于CUBS数据集，将手动标注的管腔-内膜与中膜-外膜边界转换为密集内中膜掩膜进行监督分割；引入临床变量构建辅助风险预测分支；采用蒙特卡洛dropout实现不确定性感知推理。

Result: 在1522训练/326验证/328测试图像上，LI-MA掩膜分割Dice系数0.7930，分割损失0.2359，风险预测AUC为0.6910。定性结果展示预测掩膜与手动标注基本对齐，不确定性图突出显示模糊管壁边界区域。

Conclusion: 超声衍生的颈动脉形态学可支持自动化血管壁分析与不确定性感知解释，作为可复现的形态学驱动基线，未来将纳入Doppler血流谱、血管重建与CFD壁面剪切力生物标志物。

Abstract: Carotid atherosclerosis is a major contributor to ischemic stroke and transient ischemic attack. Conventional ultrasound assessment is commonly based on intima-media thickness, plaque appearance, stenosis degree, and peak systolic velocity, but these morphology- and velocity-based indicators may not fully capture patient-specific vascular risk. This study presents AtheroFlow-XNet, a CUBS-compatible ultrasound morphology and uncertainty-aware learning baseline for carotid intima-media segmentation and preliminary risk prediction. Using the Carotid Ultrasound Boundary Study dataset, manual lumen-intima and media-adventitia boundary annotations were converted into dense intima-media masks for supervised segmentation. Clinical variables were incorporated into an auxiliary risk-prediction branch, and Monte Carlo dropout was used for uncertainty-aware inference. The model was evaluated using a patient-level train-validation-test split with 1,522 training images, 326 validation images, and 328 testing images. The proposed model achieved a Dice coefficient of 0.7930 for LI-MA mask segmentation, a segmentation loss of 0.2359, and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.6910 for preliminary risk prediction. Qualitative results showed that predicted masks were generally aligned with manual annotations, while uncertainty maps highlighted ambiguous wall-boundary regions. These results suggest that ultrasound-derived carotid morphology can support automated wall analysis and uncertainty-aware interpretation. Since CUBS does not provide Doppler waveforms or CFD-derived hemodynamic biomarkers, this work should be interpreted as a reproducible morphology-driven baseline. Future work will incorporate Doppler-derived flow profiles, patient-specific vascular reconstruction, and CFD-based wall shear biomarkers.

</details>


### [91] [Evo-Depth: A Lightweight Depth-Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14950)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Jiting Liu,Nuobei Zhu,Yunhe Li,Yuqian Fu,Yinxinyu Chen,Hongyi Cai,Zewei Ye,Bing Cheng,Kai Ye,Yiran Mao,Yilei Zhong,MingKang Dong,Junchi Yan,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evo-Depth是一个轻量级深度增强的VLA框架，通过隐式深度编码模块从多视角RGB图像提取紧凑深度特征，并利用渐进对齐训练策略提升空间感知操作能力，仅0.9B参数即在仿真和真实实验中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在需要精确空间理解的场景中表现不佳，因为主要依赖缺乏深度信息的2D视觉表征。引入显式3D输入（如深度图、点云）会增加系统复杂度、额外传感器负担，且易受噪声和重建误差影响；而隐式3D方法依赖大型几何基础模型，训练和部署成本高。

Method: 提出Evo-Depth框架，包含：(1) 轻量级隐式深度编码模块，从多视角RGB图像提取紧凑深度特征；(2) 空间增强模块，通过深度感知调制将深度特征融入视觉-语言表征；(3) 渐进对齐训练策略，对齐深度增强表征与下游动作学习。

Result: 仅0.9B参数，在四个仿真基准上取得优越性能；真实实验中达到最高平均成功率，同时模型尺寸最小、GPU内存占用最低、推理频率最高。

Conclusion: Evo-Depth在无需额外传感硬件和不牺牲部署效率的前提下，有效增强了VLA模型的空间感知操作能力，实现了性能与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by unifying perception, language grounding, and action generation. However, they often struggle in scenarios requiring precise spatial understanding, as current VLA models primarily rely on 2D visual representations that lack depth information and detailed spatial relationships. While recent approaches incorporate explicit 3D inputs such as depth maps or point clouds to address this issue, they often increase system complexity, require additional sensors, and remain vulnerable to sensing noise and reconstruction errors. Another line of work explores implicit 3D-aware spatial modeling directly from RGB observations without extra sensors, but it often relies on large geometry foundation models, resulting in higher training and deployment costs. To address these challenges, we propose Evo-Depth, a lightweight depth-enhanced VLA framework that enhances spatially grounded manipulation without relying on additional sensing hardware or compromising deployment efficiency. Evo-Depth employs a lightweight Implicit Depth Encoding Module to extract compact depth features from multi-view RGB images. These features are incorporated into vision-language representations through a Spatial Enhancement Module via depth-aware modulation, enabling efficient spatial-semantic enhancement. A Progressive Alignment Training strategy is further introduced to align the resulting depth-enhanced representations with downstream action learning. With only 0.9B parameters, Evo-Depth achieves superior performance across four simulation benchmarks. In real-world experiments, Evo-Depth attains the highest average success rate while also exhibiting the smallest model size, lowest GPU memory usage, and highest inference frequency among compared methods.

</details>


### [92] [H-OmniStereo: Zero-Shot Omnidirectional Stereo Matching with Heading-Aligned Normal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14963)
*Chenxing Jiang,Zhe Tong,Pusen Gao,Peize Liu,Yang Xu,Chuan Fang,Ping Tan,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出H-OmniStereo，一种零样本全景立体匹配框架。通过构建超过280万对顶底等距柱状立体图像数据集，并引入方位对齐坐标系下的等距柱状单目法线估计器，解决了全景立体匹配中数据稀缺和球面畸变导致单目先验退化的问题，在域外数据集和真实消费级相机上取得了更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶底等距柱状图像的立体匹配方法受限于全景立体数据集的稀缺性，以及球面畸变导致透视单目先验退化，性能严重受限。

Method: 1) 构建了超过280万对顶底等距柱状立体图像的高质量合成数据集；2) 提出方位对齐坐标系下的等距柱状单目法线估计器，提供抗畸变且跨视图一致的几何先验，提升训练效率并适应训练-测试视场角不匹配问题。

Result: 在域外数据集上实现了比现有方法更高的精度，并成功泛化到使用单一模型的实际消费级相机设置中。

Conclusion: H-OmniStereo有效解决了全景立体匹配中的数据稀缺和畸变问题，具有强泛化能力，模型与数据集将开源。

Abstract: Stereo matching on top-bottom equirectangular images provides an effective framework for full-surround perception, as vertically aligned epipolar lines enable the use of advanced perspective stereo architectures that are largely driven by large-scale datasets and monocular priors. However, the performance of such adaptations is severely limited by the scarcity of omnidirectional stereo datasets and the degradation of perspective monocular priors under spherical distortions.To address these challenges, we propose H-OmniStereo, a zero-shot omnidirectional stereo matching framework. First, we construct high-quality synthetic dataset comprising over 2.8 million top-bottom equirectangular stereo pairs to scale up training. Second, we introduce an equirectangular monocular normal estimator, specifically operating in a heading-aligned coordinate system. Beyond providing distortion-robust and cross-view-consistent geometric priors for establishing reliable correspondences in stereo matching, this design boosts training efficiency and accommodates train-test FoV mismatches.Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves higher accuracy than existing methods on out-of-domain datasets and successfully generalizes to real-world consumer camera setups using a single model. Both the model and the dataset will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [93] [MHSA: A Lightweight Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14966)
*Wei Ding,Yilin Li,Yudong Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Xingwu Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MHSA提出了一种轻量级框架，通过训练三层MLP生成器来修正LVLM中的跨模态注意力模式，在不修改模型参数的情况下有效缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作DHCP仅从跨模态注意力角度检测幻觉，未解决幻觉缓解问题。LVLM在实际应用中仍普遍存在生成内容与视觉输入不一致的幻觉现象，亟需有效的缓解方法。

Method: 训练一个简单的三层MLP生成器，以DHCP鉴别器和LVLM自身的监督信号为指导，学习生成修正后的跨模态注意力。推理时直接用修正注意力替换原始跨模态注意力，不修改任何LVLM参数。

Result: 在多种数据集和LVLM上，MHSA能同时缓解判别性幻觉和生成性幻觉。

Conclusion: 将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解，为LVLM幻觉研究提供了新视角，有助于增强模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the visual input. Prior work DHCP (Detecting Hallucinations by Cross-modal Attention Pattern) has explored hallucination detection from the perspective of cross-modal attention, but does not address hallucination mitigation. In this paper, we propose MHSA (Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention), a lightweight framework that mitigates hallucinations by learning to correct cross-modal attention patterns in LVLMs. MHSA trains a simple three-layer MLP generator to produce corrected attention, guided by supervisory signals from the DHCP discriminator and the LVLM itself. During inference, MHSA mitigates both discriminative and generative hallucinations across various datasets and LVLMs by simply replacing the original cross-modal attention with the corrected one, without modifying any LVLM parameters. By extending cross-modal attention mechanisms from hallucination detection to hallucination mitigation, MHSA offers a novel perspective on hallucination research in LVLMs and helps enhance their reliability.

</details>


### [94] [MicroscopyMatching: Towards a Ready-to-use Framework for Microscopy Image Analysis in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14980)
*Xiaofei Hui,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Shuohong Wang,Jeff W. Lichtman,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroscopyMatching，首个即用型显微图像分析框架，通过将分割、跟踪、计数等多样分析任务统一重构为匹配问题，并利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力，实现对不同显微分析场景的可靠分析，无需针对不同设置进行繁琐适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像分析对生物医学研究至关重要，但手动分析成本高、耗时长。现有深度学习方法因显微分析设置（如物体类型、实验方案、成像设备、分析任务等）高度多样化而难以泛化，通常需要大量适配工作，给实验室带来难以承受的负担，导致生物医学研究者仍普遍依赖手动分析，严重制约研究进展。因此，迫切需要一种可靠且广泛适用的显微图像分析工具。

Method: MicroscopyMatching从一个根本不同的视角出发，将多种显微图像分析任务（分割、跟踪、计数）统一重构为一个匹配问题，并利用预训练潜在扩散模型固有的鲁棒匹配能力来有效处理该问题。

Result: 该框架是首个即用型显微图像分析框架，能够在多种显微分析设置下可靠地执行分割、跟踪和计数等关键分析任务，无需针对不同场景进行大量适配。

Conclusion: MicroscopyMatching通过统一的任务重构和预训练扩散模型的匹配能力，有效解决了显微图像分析中因场景多样性导致的泛化难题，为生物医学研究提供了一种广泛适用的自动化分析工具，有望大幅提升研究效率。

Abstract: Analyzing microscopy images to extract biological object properties (e.g., their morphological organization, temporal dynamics, and population density) is fundamental to various biomedical research. Yet conducting this manually is costly and time-consuming. Though deep learning-based approaches have been explored to automate this process, the substantial diversity of microscopy analysis settings in practice (including variations of biological object types, sample processing protocols, imaging equipment, and analysis tasks, etc.) often renders them ineffective. As a result, these approaches typically require extensive adaptation for different settings, which, however, can impose burdens that are often practically unsustainable for laboratories, forcing biomedical researchers to still commonly rely on manual analysis, thereby severely bottlenecking the pace of biomedical research progress. This situation has created a pressing and long-standing need for a reliable and broadly applicable microscopy image analysis tool, yet such a tool is still missing. To address this gap, we present the first ready-to-use microscopy image analysis framework, MicroscopyMatching, that can reliably perform key analysis tasks (including segmentation, tracking, and counting) across diverse microscopy analysis settings. From a fundamentally different perspective, MicroscopyMatching reformulates diverse microscopy image analysis tasks as a unified matching problem, effectively handling this problem by exploiting the robust matching capability from pre-trained latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [95] [Compositional Video Generation via Inference-Time Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14988)
*Ariel Shaulov,Eitan Shaar,Amit Edenzon,Gal Chechik,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CVG方法，利用冻结文本到视频模型内部的交叉注意力图作为接地信号，训练轻量级组合分类器，在推理阶段引导去噪过程，提升组合语义的忠实度，无需修改模型架构或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频扩散模型在需要细粒度组合理解（如实体间关系、属性、动作、运动方向）的提示上常失败，现有方法需重新训练生成器或依赖用户提供的布局/框等额外控制，成本高且不灵活。

Method: 提出CVG（Compositional Video Guidance），一种推理时引导方法。关键观察：交叉注意力图已编码提示概念在时空上的接地信息。训练一个轻量级组合分类器，基于这些注意力特征，在早期去噪步骤中使用其梯度引导潜在轨迹朝向期望组合。分类器基于冻结的VLM骨干，可迁移至语义相关的组合标签。

Result: 在组合文本到视频基准上，CVG提升了提示忠实度，同时保留了底层生成器的视觉质量。

Conclusion: CVG无需修改模型架构、微调生成器或用户提供的控制信号，即可有效提升视频扩散模型在组合性任务上的表现，验证了利用模型自身内部信号进行推理时引导的可行性。

Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion models generate realistic videos, but often fail on prompts requiring fine-grained compositional understanding, such as relations between entities, attributes, actions, and motion directions. We hypothesize that these failures need not be addressed by retraining the generator, but can instead be mitigated by steering the denoising process using the model's own internal grounding signals. We propose \textbf{CVG}, an inference-time guidance method for improving compositional faithfulness in frozen text-to-video models. Our key observation is that cross-attention maps already encode how prompt concepts are grounded across space and time. We train a lightweight compositional classifier on these attention features and use its gradients during early denoising steps to steer the latent trajectory toward the desired composition. Built on a frozen VLM backbone, the classifier transfers across semantically related composition labels rather than relying only on narrow category-specific features. CVG improves compositional generation without modifying the model architecture, fine-tuning the generator, or requiring layouts, boxes, or other user-supplied controls. Experiments on compositional text-to-video benchmarks show improved prompt faithfulness while preserving the visual quality of the underlying generator.

</details>


### [96] [Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991)
*Francesco Pastori,Francesca Fati,Marina Rosanu,Luigi De Vitis,Lucia Ribero,Gabriella Schivardi,Giovanni Damiano Aletti,Nicoletta Colombo,Jvan Casarin,Francesco Multinu,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预处理增强CT的深度学习框架，通过自动提取3D病灶掩膜预测卵巢癌新辅助化疗反应，在280例患者队列上达到ROC-AUC 0.73和F1-score 0.70。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤，约60%患者诊断时已处于晚期，5年生存率仅约30%。早期识别新辅助化疗无应答者是一个关键的未满足临床需求，可避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机。

Method: 提出非侵入性深度学习框架：从治疗前增强CT中自动推导3D病灶掩膜；采用部分微调的预训练图像编码器处理轴向切片；通过注意力模块将切片级表征聚合为体积级嵌入；结合分类损失、监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘来区分应答者与无应答者。

Result: 在米兰欧洲肿瘤研究所280例患者（147应答，133无应答）的单中心回顾性队列上，测试集ROC-AUC为0.73（95% CI: 0.58-0.86），F1-score为0.70（95% CI: 0.56-0.82）。

Conclusion: 该架构能够学习临床相关的预测模式，为基于影像的分层工具提供了稳健基础，有望实现治疗前无创预测化疗反应。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the most lethal gynecologic malignancy: around 60% of patients are diagnosed at an advanced stage, with an associated 5-year survival rate of about 30%. Early identification of non-responders to neoadjuvant chemotherapy remains a key unmet need, as it could prevent ineffective therapy and avoid delays in optimal surgical management. This work proposes a non-invasive deep learning framework to predict neoadjuvant chemotherapy response from pre-treatment contrast-enhanced CT by leveraging automatically derived 3D lesion masks. The approach encodes axial slices with a partially fine-tuned pretrained image encoder and aggregates slice-level representations into a volumetric embedding through an attention-based module. Training combines classification loss with supervised contrastive regularization and hard-negative mining to improve separation between ambiguous responders and non-responders. The method was developed on a retrospective single-center cohort from the European Institute of Oncology (Milan, IT), including 280 eligible patients (147 responder, 133 non-responder). On the test cohort, the model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.58-0.86) and an F1-score of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.56-0.82). Overall, these results suggest that the proposed architecture learns clinically relevant predictive patterns and provides a robust foundation for an imaging-based stratification tool.

</details>


### [97] [3D Skew-Normal Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15010)
*Xiangru Wu,Ke Fan,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS)，采用偏斜正态分布作为3D高斯溅射的基本基元，通过引入可学习的偏斜参数实现从对称高斯到半高斯的连续形状插值，并在保持解析可跟踪性的同时提升重建质量，特别是在尖锐边界和薄结构上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3D高斯溅射中的高斯基元由于对称形状限制，在表示非对称结构（如物体边界、单侧表面）时不够紧凑。现有改进方法要么仍限于椭圆分布族，要么依赖硬截断导致形状控制不连续。因此需要一种能够连续控制基元形状、同时保持解析可跟踪性的新型概率分布基元。

Method: 采用Azzalini Skew-Normal分布作为基本基元，引入可学习且有界的偏斜参数实现对称高斯与半高斯的连续插值；保持仿射变换和边缘化下的解析可跟踪性以兼容现有光栅化管线；提出解耦参数化和分块优化策略解决尺度、旋转与偏斜参数的强耦合问题。

Result: 在标准新视角合成基准上，SNS在重建质量上一致优于高斯及近期非高斯核方法，在尖锐边界、薄结构和单侧表面结构上尤其表现出更清晰的收益。

Conclusion: Skew-Normal Splatting通过引入偏斜正态分布基元，在不破坏解析可跟踪性和管线兼容性的前提下，显著提升了3DGS在非对称结构上的表示紧凑性和重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis and been widely adopted in various downstream applications. The core strength of 3DGS lies in its efficient kernel-based scene representation, where Gaussian primitives provide favorable mathematical and computational properties. However, under a finite primitive budget, the symmetric shape of each primitive directly affects representation compactness, especially near asymmetric structures such as object boundaries and one-sided surfaces. Recent works have explored more complex kernel distributions, yet they either remain within the elliptical family or rely on hard truncation, which limits continuous shape control and introduces distributional discontinuities. In this paper, we propose Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS), which adopts the Azzalini Skew-Normal distribution as the fundamental primitive. By introducing a learnable and bounded skewness parameter, SNS can continuously interpolate between symmetric Gaussians and Half-Gaussian-like shapes, enabling flexible modeling of both sharp boundaries and interior regions. Moremover, SNS preserves analytical tractability under affine transformations and marginalization. This property allows seamless integration into existing Gaussian Splatting rasterization pipelines.Furthermore, to address the strong coupling between scale, rotation, and skewness parameters, we introduce a decoupled parameterization and a block-wise optimization strategy to enhance training stability and accuracy. Extensive experiments on standard novel-view synthesis benchmarks show that SNS consistently improves reconstruction quality over Gaussian and recent non-Gaussian kernels, with clearer benefits on sharp boundaries and thin or one-sided structures.

</details>


### [98] [EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042)
*Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Mariam Hassan,Lan Feng,Wentao Pan,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EverAnimate是一种高效的后训练方法，通过持久潜在上下文记忆机制（包括持久潜在传播和恢复性流匹配）解决长时动画视频生成中的质量漂移和语义漂移问题，在10秒和90秒设定下均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时动画生成面临两大挑战：(i) 低层质量漂移（如静态背景逐步退化）和(ii) 高层语义漂移（如角色身份和视角相关属性不一致），现有分块生成方法因累积漂移而效果不佳。

Method: 提出两种互补机制：(i) 持久潜在传播（Persistent Latent Propagation）在分块间维护上下文记忆以传播身份和运动信息，缓解时间遗忘；(ii) 恢复性流匹配（Restorative Flow Matching）通过采样过程中的速度调整引入隐式恢复目标，提升分块内保真度。仅需轻量级LoRA微调。

Result: 在10秒设定下，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒设定下，增益扩大至15%/15%和32%/27%，全面超越现有长动画生成方法。

Conclusion: EverAnimate通过持久潜在上下文记忆有效解决了长时动画生成中的累积漂移问题，是一种高效且效果显著的轻量级后训练方法。

Abstract: We propose EverAnimate, an efficient post-training method for long-horizon animated video generation that preserves visual quality and character identity. Long-form animation remains challenging because highly dynamic human motion must be synthesized against relatively static environments, making chunk-based generation prone to accumulated drift: (i) low-level quality drift, such as progressive degradation of static backgrounds, and (ii) high-level semantic drift, such as inconsistent character identity and view-dependent attributes. To address this issue, EverAnimate restores drifted flow trajectories by anchoring generation to a persistent latent context memory, consisting of two complementary mechanisms. (i) Persistent Latent Propagation maintains a context memory across chunks to propagate identity and motion in latent space while mitigating temporal forgetting. (ii) Restorative Flow Matching introduces an implicit restoration objective during sampling through velocity adjustment, improving within-chunk fidelity. With only lightweight LoRA tuning, EverAnimate outperforms state-of-the-art long-animation methods in both short- and long-horizon settings: at 10 seconds, it improves PSNR/SSIM by 8%/7% and reduces LPIPS/FID by 22%/11%; at 90 seconds, the gains increase to 15%/15% and 32%/27%, respectively.

</details>


### [99] [LATERN: Test-Time Context-Aware Explainable Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15054)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LATERN框架，通过上下文感知异常评分（CEA）和递归证据聚合（REA）两个模块，将视频异常检测重构为时间证据聚合过程，解决视觉语言模型在片段级独立推理中缺乏结构化时间上下文的问题，在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等基准上提升了检测精度和解释一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的视频异常检测方法受限于token约束，只能进行片段级独立推理，缺乏结构化时间上下文，导致预测和解释碎片化。本文旨在让VLM能够将异常理解为偏离演化视频动态的变化，而非产生孤立的预测。

Method: 提出LATERN框架，包含两个互补模块：1) 上下文感知异常评分（CEA），通过图像支撑的记忆机制，利用帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐选择性选择历史内容作为扩展上下文，生成可靠异常分数；2) 递归证据聚合（REA），基于异常分数执行递归时间聚合，识别连贯的异常区间，生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等具有挑战性的基准上，LATERN在测试时提升了冻结视觉语言模型的检测精度和解释一致性，生成了时间连贯且语义有据的事件级解释。

Conclusion: LATERN通过将视频异常检测重构为时间证据聚合过程，有效解决了VLM在VAD任务中缺乏时间上下文的问题，同时保持了模型的可解释性，为视频异常检测提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for video anomaly detection (VAD) due to their strong visual reasoning ability and natural language-based explainability. In this paper, we aim to address a key limitation of such pipelines, which perform segment-level inference independently owing to token constraints and reason without structured temporal context, allowing VLMs to interpret anomalies as deviations from evolving video dynamics rather than producing fragmented predictions and explanations. To specify, we propose a context-aware framework named LATERN, which reformulates VAD as a temporal evidence aggregation process. LATERN consists of two complementary modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA) and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA). CEA introduces a novel image-grounded memory mechanism, which selectively chooses historical content via frame diversity and visual-textual alignment as expanded context to help generate reliable anomaly scores. Building upon these scores, REA performs recursive temporal aggregation to identify coherent anomaly intervals and produce event-level decisions and explanations grounded in visual-textual evidence. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks, including UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, show that LATERN enhances detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs during test time, while generating temporally coherent and semantically grounded event-level explanations.

</details>


### [100] [Computational Imaging Priors for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy: Monte Carlo-Guided Hemoglobin Mapping for Rare-Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15062)
*Chengshuai Yang,Lei Xing,Gregory Entin,Roopa Vemulapalli,Lisa Casey,Raiyan Tripti Zaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种蒙特卡洛启发的解析先验模型，从RGB信号计算血红蛋白浓度，作为辅助信息(双零初始化通道融合或蒸馏头)输入胶囊内镜分类器。在Kvasir-Capsule数据集(47,238帧,11类)上，相比RGB-only EfficientNet-B0，该方法在macro-AUC上取得小幅但方向一致提升(0.760→0.783)，最大稳健类别提升在淋巴管扩张症(AUC 0.238→0.337)，酒精性血管类别的种子间方差较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB训练的胶囊内镜分类器对小血管病变检测能力不足，原因是将血红蛋白对比度与胆汁和光照衰减混淆。因此需要一种能从RGB信号中解耦血红蛋白信息的计算模型。

Method: 使用蒙特卡洛启发的解析模型从RGB信号计算血红蛋白概率P_blood = sigma(alpha * (H_norm - 0.5)) * Phi(r)，以两种方式集成：(i)输入融合——作为两个零初始化辅助通道与RGB拼接；(ii)蒸馏头——训练3通道RGB主干预测P_blood。在Kvasir-Capsule上6种随机种子进行实验。

Result: 输入融合macro-AUC 0.783 vs RGB-only 0.760 (+0.023, 5/6种子正方向); 蒸馏0.773; 淋巴管扩张症AUC从0.238提升至0.337(全部6种子一致); 血管类(血管扩张、新鲜血液)种子间呈双峰分布，种子42上血管扩张AUC从0.528跃升至0.916但跨种子均值下降。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛启发的解析先验在Kvasir-Capsule上提供小幅但方向一致的macro-AUC提升，最大稳健类别收益在淋巴管扩张症；蒸馏变体可在纯3通道RGB上运行并提供免费的可解释性热力图。

Abstract: Background. RGB-trained capsule-endoscopy classifiers underperform on small-vessel vascular findings by
  conflating hemoglobin contrast with bile and illumination falloff. Thus, here we test whether a Monte
  Carlo-inspired analytic model can compute hemoglobin from RGB signal built upon extracted classifier.
  Methods. On Kvasir-Capsule (47,238 frames, video-level 70/15/15 split, 11 evaluable classes) we evaluate two
  software-only configurations against RGB-only EfficientNet-B0 across 6 seeds: (i) a prior P_blood =
  sigma(alpha * (H_norm - 0.5)) * Phi(r) fused as 2 zero-init auxiliary channels; (ii) a distillation head
  training a 3-channel RGB backbone to predict P_blood. Significance: paired DeLong, McNemar, bootstrap CIs
  with Bonferroni correction.
  Results. Across 6 seeds (n=6,423), the analytic prior provides a small but direction-consistent macro-AUC
  improvement: RGB-only 0.760 +/- 0.027, input-fusion 0.783 +/- 0.024 (paired Delta = +0.023, sign-positive on
  5/6 seeds), distillation 0.773 +/- 0.028. The largest robust per-class lift is on Lymphangiectasia, where AUC
  rises from RGB 0.238 +/- 0.057 to input-fusion 0.337 +/- 0.019, sign-consistent across all 6 seeds. On rare
  focal-vascular classes (Angiectasia, Blood - fresh) the prior's per-seed effects are bimodal: seed=42 reaches
  Angiectasia AUC 0.528 -> 0.916, but the cross-seed mean is 0.646 -> 0.608 with sigma_PI = 0.23 - reported as
  a high-variance per-seed exemplar.
  Conclusion. A Monte Carlo-inspired analytic prior provides a small, direction-consistent macro-AUC
  improvement on Kvasir-Capsule across 6 seeds with the largest robust per-class lift on Lymphangiectasia; the
  distillation variant runs on plain 3-channel RGB and yields a free interpretability heatmap.

</details>


### [101] [CoralLite: μCT Reconstruction of Coral Colonies from Individual Corallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15093)
*Jess Jones,Leonardo Bertini,Kenneth Johnson,Erica Hendy,Tilo Burghardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoralLite数据集和基于混合V-Trans-UNet架构的深度学习基线，用于从μCT扫描中重建珊瑚骨骼中每个珊瑚个体的三维模型，实现了首个全自动的珊瑚个体分割与三维建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 珊瑚群落的生命史记录在骨骼结构中，但活体组织仅为一薄层，需追踪每个珊瑚个体周围的骨骼沉积以理解息肉分裂速率、时间及其对群落骨骼生长的影响。目前缺乏从μCT扫描中自动重建单个珊瑚个体三维模型的方法。

Method: 构建CoralLite数据集（697张μCT切片、37张部分或完整切片标注）；提出混合V-Trans-UNet架构，先在弱标注数据上预训练，再在8000+手动标注的珊瑚个体区域上进行拓扑感知微调；实现跨切片链接的每个珊瑚个体三维模型可视化。

Result: 在同一群落未见切片上达到0.94拓扑准确率、平均Dice 0.77；在无生物学关联的独立标本上平均Dice 0.63。首次证明视觉机器学习可有效从μCT扫描实现全3D个体珊瑚建模。

Conclusion: 实验虽在规模与场景上有限，但结果表明深度学习方法能支持从珊瑚骨骼μCT扫描中自动进行完整的3D个体珊瑚建模，为未来研究提供了基准数据集和开源代码。

Abstract: The life history of an individual coral is archived within the accreting skeleton of the colony. While reef-forming coral colonies (e.g. massive \emph{Porites} sp.) may live for hundreds of years and deposit calcareous structures many metres in height and width, their living tissue is a thin outer surface layer comprised of asexually-dividing polyps that only survive a few years. To understand the rate and timing of polyp division and the consequences for colony skeletal growth, scientists need to track the skeletal corallite deposited around each polyp. Here we propose CoralLite, an annotated μCT scan dataset of entire calcareous skeletons and an associated, first corallite deep learning reconstruction baseline. CoralLite combines fully quantified volumetric segmentations with cross-slice linking for visualisations of 3D models for each corallite up to colony scale. For segmentation, we propose and evaluate in detail a hybrid V-Trans-UNet architecture applicable to segmenting tiled μCT virtual slabs of \emph{Porites} sp. colonies. The model is pre-trained on weakly annotated data and topology-aware fine-tuned using fully annotated slice sections with 8k+ manual corallite region annotations. On unseen slices of the same colony, the resulting model reaches 0.94 topological accuracy at mean Dice scores of 0.77 on the same colony and projection axis, and 0.63 mean Dice scores on a different, biologically unrelated specimen. Whilst our experiments are limited in scale and context, our results show for the first time that visual machine learning can effectively support full 3D individual corallite modelling from μCT scans of coral skeletons alone. For reproducibility and as a baseline for future research we publish our full dataset of 697 μCT slices, 37 partial or full slice annotations, and all network weights and source code with this paper.

</details>


### [102] [DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15116)
*Haonan Zhao,Yiting Wang,Jingkun Chen,Valentina Donzella,Thomas Bashford-Rogers,Kurt Debattista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveCtrl是一个基于深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成框架，通过结构感知适配器保持场景布局和运动模式，并引入可扩展数据生成管道和驾驶视频真实感评分（DVRS）指标，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成中的仿真到真实差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对训练自动驾驶系统至关重要，但仿真数据与真实世界视频之间存在域差距，现有视频生成方法无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性和视觉真实感，限制了仿真数据的下游部署效用。

Method: 基于预训练视频基础模型，提出结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成；构建可扩展数据生成管道，支持结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示三种条件信号；提出驾驶领域知识驱动的DVRS评估指标。

Result: 在真实感、时间质量和感知任务性能上均优于基础模型和竞争方法，显著缩小了仿真到真实的域差距。

Conclusion: DriveCtrl通过深度条件可控生成有效解决了仿真驾驶视频到真实视频的域迁移问题，生成的视频在保持原始标注有效性的同时具备高真实感和时间一致性。

Abstract: Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.

</details>


### [103] [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Kaiwen Zheng,Zihan Zhou,Bokai Yan,Xinyuan Li,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Forcing++，一种用于逐帧自回归视频生成的高效初始化流水线，通过因果一致性蒸馏实现1-2步采样，在帧级2步设置下超越SOTA的4步方法，同时减少50%首帧延迟和约4倍训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散蒸馏方法受限于粗粒度的响应和不可忽视的采样延迟，在帧级自回归（仅1-2步采样）这一更具挑战性的设置下，现有初始化策略存在目标不对齐、无法支持少步生成或扩展成本过高等瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出Causal Forcing++流水线，核心是因果一致性蒸馏（causal CD）：学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，但仅需从相邻时间步之间的单步在线教师ODE获取监督，避免预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹，从而实现高效且易于优化的初始化。

Result: 在帧级2步设置下，VBench Total提升0.1，VBench Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335，首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本降低约4倍。此外还将流水线扩展到以动作为条件的世界模型生成（类似Genie3）。

Conclusion: Causal Forcing++证明了在极少量步（1-2步）的逐帧自回归设置下，通过因果一致性蒸馏进行初始化是高效且可扩展的方案，显著优于现有的4步分块自回归方法，并为实时交互式视频生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

</details>


### [104] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究纯合成分层数据是否能提升平面设计分解效果。基于CLD基线构建SynLayers合成数据集，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督和自动推理边界框。研究发现：(1)纯合成数据训练可超越非可扩展方案；(2)性能随数据量提升，约50K样本时趋于饱和；(3)合成数据能实现层数分布的均衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成输出为扁平化结果，前景、背景和文本纠缠在固定画布中，导致后期编辑困难。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么从有限的结构先验中构建部分合成数据，均面临可扩展性挑战。作者探索纯合成分层数据能否解决这一"最后一公里"问题。

Method: 基于CLD（最先进的图层分解框架）基线，构建合成数据集SynLayers，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成文本监督信息，并利用VLM预测的边界框自动生成推理输入。通过数据驱动的研究范式，系统性地评估纯合成数据在平面设计分解中的效果。

Result: (1)纯合成数据训练效果超过非可扩展的PrismLayersPro等真实数据集；(2)性能随训练数据量增加而提升，约50K样本时增益趋于饱和；(3)合成数据可避免真实数据集中常见的层数分布不平衡问题，实现更均衡的层数控制。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据可作为平面设计分解任务中可扩展且有效的替代方案，有望推动合成数据在分层设计编辑系统中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

</details>


### [105] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviScreen，一个利用历史病例区域级证据进行疾病筛查的推理论证框架，通过双知识库检索实现回溯可解释性，并基于证据感知推理和对比检索异常图提升筛查性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像筛查模型可解释性有限、性能欠佳，缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例或提供透明的推理路径。

Method: EviScreen包含两个核心模块：1）区域级证据检索模块，从双知识库（双知识银行）中检索历史病例的区域证据，实现回溯可解释性；2）证据感知推理模块，结合当前病例与历史证据进行预测；此外，利用对比检索生成异常图替代传统的后验显著性图，增强定位可解释性。

Result: 在真实疾病筛查基准上取得优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过证据驱动的推理机制有效提升了疾病筛查的性能与可解释性，为医学影像筛查提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

</details>


### [106] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM是一个26亿参数的开源世界模型，支持一分钟视频生成、720p分辨率、6自由度相机控制，在保持高质量的同时实现36倍吞吐量提升，蒸馏变体可在单张RTX 5090上34秒生成60秒720p视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长时程高保真视频生成方面存在效率瓶颈，缺乏同时支持分钟级时长、高分辨率、精确相机控制的开源解决方案。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：帧级门控DeltaNet与softmax注意力结合，实现长上下文高效建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：精确6自由度轨迹跟随；(3) 两阶段生成流水线：长视频精炼器提升质量与一致性；(4) 鲁棒标注流水线：从公开视频提取精确度量级6自由度相机位姿。

Result: 仅用约21.3万公开视频片段完成训练，在64块H100上15天完成训练，单GPU生成60秒视频；吞吐量比现有开源基线高36倍，蒸馏变体在RTX 5090上34秒生成60秒720p视频。

Conclusion: SANA-WM在分钟级世界建模任务中实现了动作跟随精度和视觉质量的最优平衡，显著提升了可扩展世界模型的效率。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control.
